## paragraph:4282

Kolon bağımlılıkları ve güvenli serileştirme

## paragraph:4283

closed_row: Per profile row exactly selected columns plus required fieldStates object mapping selected nullable column names to KNOWN|ABSENT|UNAVAILABLE. fieldStates keys exactly nullable selected columns, no hidden existence metadata. Required nonnullable missing fails source population; nullable source unknown emits null/UNAVAILABLE, authoritative no-record null/ABSENT, known value KNOWN. A nullable field denied is not exported as unknown: entire job403 before materialization. No WITHHELD counts, no masked participant subsets. Server rows sorted id ASC only.

## paragraph:4284

serializers: UUID canonical lowercase; Instant UTC ISO8601 milliseconds; Date YYYY-MM-DD; IANA exact source zone; Qty/SignedQty lossless decimal string native63 scale/bounds, never IEEE float; Money is native63 decimal(19,4) string paired with separate currencyId field from same currencyRef. No JSON tuple or floating point conversion. Nullable null is empty CSV/XLSX cell with corresponding fieldState in explicit companion column; PDF uses Bilinmiyor or Kaynakta kayıt yok from state, not0. Text escaped for CSV/XLSX formula injection; no tracking URL resolution. Nullable column companion state counts against dataset column limit. Selecting lastPrice requires currencyId as mandatory selected companion, or validation422; both source grants required. No new cost computation.

## paragraph:4285

column_dependency_rule: Apply only keys present in selected profile. Any selected numeric value column requires every declared companion column also selected and source-authorized; missing selection422 COLUMN_DEPENDENCY_REQUIRED before snapshot, unauthorized companion403 no file. Do not silently add unauthorized columns. Known numeric source value with missing currency/unit source is null UNAVAILABLE, never naked number; genuine null values retain exact source absence/unavailability. Unit must exact owner record unit; no latest-master inference/conversion. CurrencyId exact source ISO4217 currencyRef identity, not arbitrary UUID label. Explicit companion metadata schema binds ISO4217 source currency code in dataset column label/format metadata only if current authorized; unknown metadata blocks price interpretation. READINESS uses exact stored assessment unit, BALANCES source current base unit. No aggregation across units/currencies.

## paragraph:4286

column_dependencies: {"lastPrice":["currencyId"],"requiredQty":["unitId"],"reservedQty":["unitId"],"availableQty":["unitId"],"thisRequesterReservedUsable":["unitId"],"evaluationQuantity":["unitId"],"shortageQty":["unitId"],"minStock":["unitId"],"reorderPoint":["unitId"],"lastCountVarianceQty":["unitId","lastCountDate","lastCountZone"]}

## paragraph:4287

permission: Original human reporting.run.export + reporting.artifact.download at download, exact report/dataset read/export rights, profile ownerRead plus actual source row/field/purpose. PART price/date/currency and ORDERS commercial require stock.commercial.read; quantities and count variance stock.quantity.read; person/current assignment metadata require exact source rights. All referenced source metadata checks conjunctive. New source fields require exact FieldDefinition READ+EXPORT mapping in registered dataset; no wildcard or implicit grant. Registered service actor alone cannot substitute original human.

## paragraph:4288

Kaynak adaptörü ve atomik yayın

## paragraph:4289

OpenSnapshot: {profileId:const TSP_SUMMARY_EXPORT_V2,runId:UUID,runVersion:TspRunVersion,parameters:closed above,sourceCutRef:TspSourceCutRef,expectedSchemaRef:registered IAM RevisionRef,trustedActor:{principalId:UUID,personId:UUID,authorizationEpoch:AuthEpoch,purpose:TSP_REPORT_EXPORT},registeredServiceId:UUID}. Returns closed COMPLETE {snapshotRef:TspSnapshotRef,sourceManifestHash:Hash64,schemaRef:IAM RevisionRef,cut:exact SourceCutRecord values,rowCount:int0..1000,fieldPaths:exact requested paths,sourceFenceRef:TspSourceFenceRefV1} or safe native SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE/SOURCE_CHANGED/FORBIDDEN errors. Complete authoritative selected root population required; rowCount private not public count oracle. Snapshot private immutable member/source cut map pins every requested root/current revision/source input; no remote arbitrary SQL.

## paragraph:4290

ReadSnapshotPage: {snapshotRef,sourceManifestHash,runId,runVersion,cursor:opaqueText2048|null,limit:integer1..100,trustedActor,registeredServiceId}; closed {items:typed selected profile row[0..limit],nextCursor:opaqueText2048|null,sourceManifestHash}. Cursor binds snapshot/schema/profile/field paths/actor/epoch/lastId; first/next current rights checks. Terminal chain and exact rowCount verified before publication; missing duplicate page fails. No new public API.

## paragraph:4291

WithExportSourceFence: Existing55 three-way source protocol {snapshotRef,sourceManifestHash,runId,runVersion,commandId,runPayloadHash,trustedActor,registeredServiceId}. Same authoritative transaction current source/auth/withdrawal and owner source receipt +55artifact digest/visibility commit. Response COMMITTED {runId,runVersion,commandId,runPayloadHash,commitReceiptId,committedAt}|NOT_COMMITTED {code:SOURCE_CHANGED|FORBIDDEN|SOURCE_FENCE_UNAVAILABLE}|UNKNOWN {runId,commandId}. UNIQUE tenant/runId/commandId; response loss reconciles exact existing ledger; UNKNOWN never newrun/command/callback. Same id different payload409. Atomic boundary absent503, no artifact. No stock reservation/movement/requisition/order mutation.

## paragraph:4292

source_fence_schema: NEW_DESIGN TspSourceFenceRefV1 closed {owner:const63.01,snapshotId:UUID,fenceId:UUID,fenceRevisionId:UUID}; all required/noextras. Ref identifies private immutable TspSourceFenceRecordV1 created atomically with OpenSnapshot result: {id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,snapshotId:UUID,runId:UUID,runVersion:TspRunVersion,sourceCutRef:TspSourceCutRef,sourceManifestHash:Hash64,profileRevisionRef:canonical IAM RevisionRef,schemaRevisionRef:canonical IAM RevisionRef,originalActorId:UUID,actorPersonId:UUID,registeredServiceId:UUID,privateSourceVectorRef:canonical owner63 RevRef,authorizationFencePin:TspAuthFencePinV1,createdAt:Instant}. Vector records exact selected roots/revisions/source partition manifests and original actor field/purpose policy, bounded by1000 roots and canonical source schema limit. Server allocates unique fenceId/revisionId once, UNIQUE(snapshotId,runId,runVersion); repeated same snapshot returns same record. This is not a domain-record revision cast, permission token or legal assurance. WithExportSourceFence resolves exact private record and rechecks every live authorization/source/withdrawal relation under authoritative commit lock. Missing record/unavailable boundary503; mismatched run/snapshot409; unauthorized403. Private fence ref/material never a user-facing report field. TspAuthFencePinV1 closed {principalId:UUID,personId:UUID,authorizationEpoch:AuthEpoch,sourcePolicyRevisionRefs:canonical IAM RevisionRef[1..100]}; pinned from current authenticated actor and approved source policies, not a new permission grant. Native63 privateSourceVectorRef resolves new immutable TspSourceVectorV1 closed {id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,items:TspSourceMemberPinV1[1..1000],capturedAt:Instant}; member closed {selectedRootId:UUID,selectedRootRevisionRef:native63 RevRef,contributorRefs:unique native63 RevRef[1..100],schemaRevisionRef:canonical IAM RevisionRef}. Source vector is allocated in same snapshot/fence transaction, contentHash computed from canonical exact vector body; no false native ERP revision IDs. Missing or overflowing full contributor closure fails source snapshot, no truncation. Current native authority/withdrawal predicates are revalidated against vector refs at fence, not inferred from hash equality alone.

SiPopulationProfileValueV1={schemaVersion:1, selection:SiPopulationDefinitionV1, exposure:SiExposureDefinitionV1, rationale:Text2000}. METRIC_DENOMINATOR exact aynı yayımlı metrik denominator revizyonunu ve unitCode değerini ister; başka metrikten exposure üretilemez. Bilinmeyen exposure sıfır sayılmaz.

SiPeriodProfileValueV1={schemaVersion:1, mode:EXACT_WINDOW|CALENDAR_DAY|CALENDAR_WEEK|CALENDAR_MONTH, calendar:SiCalendarDefinitionV1|null, maximumElapsedSeconds:int1..31622400, completion:COMPLETE_PERIOD_ONLY, rationale:Text2000}. Her alan zorunlu; ek alan yoktur. EXACT_WINDOW için calendar=null; diğer üç dalda doludur. weekStart yalnız WEEK hesabında kullanılır, başka dallarda örtük etki yaratmaz. Tatil/iş günü takvimi veya cihaz locale varsayımı bu tipe eklenmez.

Raporun native inceleme makbuzları gerçek inceleyen kimliğini, gerekçeyi, exact içerik hashini ve kaynak doğrulama vektörünü saklar. Serbest metin kendi başına yasal yükümlülük kanıtı kabul edilmez.

Mevcut reporting.report.validate, submit_review, approve, reject, publish, new_version, deprecate, withdraw ve archive eylem/yolları korunur. withReadFence taşıma sonucu JSON null olur; typed callback aynı güncel kaynak/yetki bariyeri içinde64 read serialization veya kayıtlı exact domain commitine kadar çalışır. ## paragraph:4325

54 anahtarları si.population.profile_ref ve si.period.profile_ref, yalnız canonical descriptor hedefli REVISION_REF değerleridir. COMPANY_AT_CUT tam olarak {companyId:bundle.companyId, selectionCut:run.effectiveAt} seçimine gider;55 COMPANY scope aynı şirkettir. RETIRED/MERGED/DRAFT dışlanır; listeye sessiz filtre uygulanmaz. Eksik hak403/404, 1000üstü422, eksik tarihsel kaynak503, çözülemeyen operator409 olur. ## paragraph:4329

Yeni source55 SiCalendarBoundaryResolverV1 salt hesaplayıcıdır. EXACT_WINDOW için requestedWindow.from<to<=trustedRunStartedAt ve elapsed<=maximumElapsedSeconds doğrulanır; otomatik yuvarlama yoktur. DAY yerel00:00’dan sonraki yerel tarihin00:00’ına; WEEK kaynak weekStart gününden yedi takvim tarihi sonrasına; MONTH ayın ilk gününden sonraki ayın ilk gününe gider. Her sınır exact zone/artifact ve gap/fold politikasından ayrı çözülür;24saat veya30gün eklenmez. Resolved Window istekle exact eşit olmalıdır.

NEXT_VALID_INSTANT nedeniyle iki sınır eşit veya ters olursa ya da bir yerel gün bütünüyle atlanırsa dönem geçersizdir. Tamamlanmamış dönem olumlu evidence üretmez. Period.to metric source effective cut olur. Baseline exact yayımlı comparison profile ve aynı period profile revizyonuna uyar;28 ve31günlük ardışık takvim ayları yalnız süre farkı yüzünden NOT_COMPARABLE yapılmaz. Unknown veya eksik timezone/parser kaynağında sistem/cihaz timezoneuna dönülmez.

## paragraph:4331

ResolveWindow kapalı isteği {profileRef:RevisionRef, requestedWindow:Window, trustedRunStartedAt:Instant}; güvenilir55 kaynak bağlamından gelir. Sonuç {state:MATCH, window:Window, profileContentHash:SHA256, tzdbArtifactHash:SHA256|null}|{state:INVALID, reason:CALENDAR_BOUNDARY|INCOMPLETE_PERIOD|PERIOD_LIMIT}|{state:UNAVAILABLE}. EXACT_WINDOW için artifactHash=null; takvim dallarında doludur. MATCH yalnız exact istek sınırları içindir; yeni run, izin veya approval üretmez. Diğer55 takvim kaynaklarına ait eski refler bu yeni tipe cast edilmez.

## paragraph:4332

TZIF_ARCHIVE_V1 ZIP veya TAR değildir; dosya sistemine çıkartılmaz. Canonical UTF8 JSON {format:TZIF_ARCHIVE_V1, release:Text32, zones:TzifZoneEntry[1..10000]}; TzifZoneEntry={name:IANA, bytesBase64:canonical padded RFC4648 base64, byteLength:int1..1048576, sha256:SHA256}. Her alan zorunlu; ek alan ve duplicate JSON key yasaktır. zones ASCII name sırasıyla benzersizdir. Name1..255 ASCII segment [A-Za-z0-9_+-]+ ve / ayırıcıdır; empty/dot/dotdot, baştaki slash, backslash, NUL, symlink/pathmetadata/URL yoktur. İsimler yalnız mantıksal zone anahtarıdır, dosya yolu değildir.

## paragraph:4333

Outer UTF8 JSON en çok104857600byte; toplam decoded zone verisi67108864byte; entry1MiB ve toplam10000entry sınırı allocation öncesinde uygulanır. Streaming parser/decode limitleri, declared byteLength ve SHA256 değerlerini exact doğrular; canonical base64 roundtrip zorunludur. Haricî dosya aliası, trailing decoded veri veya keyfi arşiv extraction yoktur. Bütün payloadlar TZif magic ve desteklenen revision yapısını sağlar. Header count/transition array taşması, bozuk yapı veya desteklenmeyen trailing yapı bütün artifactı reddeder. POSIX-TZ footer yalnız timezone kural grameriyle parse edilir, eval çalıştırılmaz.

## paragraph:4334

Uygulama Instant değeri POSIX zaman çizgisinde UTC milisaniyedir; :60 saniye yazımı reddedilir. Kabul edilen TZif verisinin her blockunda leapcnt=0 olmalıdır. right/* namespace veya herhangi leap-second tablosu reddedilir, sessizce yok sayılmaz. Leap-aware veri için ayrı sürümlü codec gerekir. Yerel bir TZif dosyasının varlığı kabul edilmiş parser veya kaynak veri olduğu anlamına gelmez.

## paragraph:4335

TzdbArtifactManifestV1={release:Text32, sha256:SHA256, format:TZIF_ARCHIVE_V1, zoneIds:IANA[1..10000], artifactByteLength:int1..104857600}; zoneIds exact archive ad kümesi ve sırasıdır. Actual immutable artifact bytes ve manifest55 deployment custodyde tutulur. Hash exact archive bytesını kapsar; ağdan lazy indirme, değişebilir path veya systemlatest fallback yoktur.

## paragraph:4336

CalendarParserAdmissionV1={id:UUID, revisionId:UUID, version:1, contentHash:SHA256, parserKey:SI_TZIF_CALENDAR_V1, buildArtifactHash:SHA256, format:TZIF_ARCHIVE_V1, supportedTzifVersions:(2|3|4)[1..3], fixtureManifestHash:SHA256, artifactManifestHash:SHA256}. TZif sürümleri unique sorted’dır. Gerçek registry kimlikleri bir kez üretilir. Kaynak55 technical reviewer ile ayrı platform/security reviewer aynı material/build/fixture/artifact hashlarını kabul eder; ikisi birbirinden ve material author/build submitter kişisinden farklıdır. Servis hesabı veya rol aliası bağımsızlığı sağlayamaz. Eksik/iptal edilmiş kabul veya parser/artifact mismatch503’tür.

## paragraph:4337

CALENDAR_PARSER_ADMISSION_HASH_V1, kendi contentHash alanı çıkarılmış kabul materyalini domain-separated {codec, material} içinde hashler. Yalnız /version hash-only canonical decimal stringe çevrilir; bounded diğer sayılar değişmez. UTF8 RFC8785 JCS SHA256 kullanılır. Fixture seti leap/nonleap, hafta başlangıcı, gap/fold, atlanan gün, bozukTZif, duplicate key ve kaynak sınırlarını kapsar; yürütülene kadar NOT_RUN’dır.

## paragraph:4338

SI_POPULATION_CHILD_HASH_V1 hash materyali: complete SiPopulationProfileRevisionV1 excluding only outer /contentHash. Kayıpsız canonical decimal string yolları: /version, /parentReportRevisionRef/version, /metricDefinitionRef/version, /value/exposure/metricDefinitionRef/version, /value/exposure/denominatorDefinitionRef/version. last two only METRIC_DENOMINATOR; absenceNOT_USED; allintegerfields positive int64 exceptconstant childversion1

## paragraph:4339

SI_PERIOD_CHILD_HASH_V1 hash materyali: complete SiPeriodProfileRevisionV1 excluding only outer /contentHash. Kayıpsız canonical decimal string yolları: /version, /parentReportRevisionRef/version, /metricDefinitionRef/version. Diğer sayısal alanlar native bounded türleriyle korunur.

## paragraph:4340

Yeni parent rapor hash codecinin adı SI_REPORT_TYPED_TREE_HASH_V2 olur. Önceki tasarımdaki SI_REPORT_BUNDLE_INPUT_HASH_V2 ve yalnız siProfileBundle Version yollarını dönüştüren parent reçetesi bu yeni kabulde kullanılmaz. Complete validated ReportDraftSiV2 gövdesi kayıpsız typed JSON ağacına çevrilir. Native ReportDraftV1 alan doğrulaması ve unknown-field reddi önce uygulanır; yeni codec arbitrary alan veya sayı kabulü açmaz. Wire türleri ve eskiV1 body/receipt/replay hashleri değiştirilmez. Child ve descriptor için yukarıdaki literal Version yolu codec kuralları aynen kalır.

## paragraph:4341

Child codec kuralı: her child hash {codec:<exact ad>, material:<typed projected body>} üzerinden UTF8 RFC8785 JCS SHA256 lowercase hex olur. Listelenen present Version değerlerinin1dahil hepsi canonical decimal stringe çevrilir; IEEE Number, wildcard veya alan adına göre dönüşüm yoktur. Descriptor ayrı SI_PROFILE_TARGET_DESCRIPTOR_HASH_V1 kuralındaki {codec,body} zarfını kullanır; child {codec,material} zarfına alınmaz. Nested source hashları korunur; yalnız belirtilen dış digest çıkarılır. Desteklenmeyen sayısal/şema durumu reddedilir. Yeni request idempotency hashı normalized şema başlığı ve native REPORT-2 request materyalini ayrı domain etiketiyle kapsar; doğrulanmış request gövdesi için aynı kayıpsız Node dönüşümü kullanılır. Request hashı ile stored body hashı farklı amaçtır. Eski rapor hashları yeniden üretilmez.

## paragraph:4342

SI_PROFILE_TARGET_DESCRIPTOR_HASH_V1, descriptor body üzerinde domain-separated {codec, body} UTF8JCS SHA256 kullanır. Hash-only decimal string yolları /nativeChildRef/version, /parentReportRevisionRef/version, /metricDefinitionRef/version ile sınırlıdır. schemaVersion1 native küçük integerdır. Nested hashlar korunur; descriptor outer contentHash materyale girmez.

## paragraph:4343

Ekran ve alan yönetimi: rapor tasarımcısında ayrı Emniyet profili bölümü yalnız ilgili alan yetkisiyle görünür. Company ve metric alanları mevcut kaynak seçicilerinden; explicit vessel tam yetkili kaynak listesinden seçilir. Seçim türü değiştiğinde eski dalın alanları taşınmaz, doğrulanmış diff gösterilir. Dönem türü EXACT_WINDOW seçilince calendar=null, takvim türlerinde zone/artifact/hafta/gap/fold alanları zorunludur. Rationale zorunludur. Read-only yayınlı profil kendi parent revisionını gösterir; düzenleme yeni report revision başlatır. Gizli field isimleri veya child sayıları öneri/seçici üzerinden sızdırılmaz.

## paragraph:4344

Yeni alan profili en az şu ayrı grupları kapsar: company/metric; population selection kind; tam vesselIds kümesi; exposure kind/ref/unit; population rationale; period mode; calendar zone/artifact/weekStart/gap/fold; maximumElapsedSeconds/completion; period rationale; protected child/descriptor metadata. Okuma, yazma ve canonical hash açıklama ayrı doğrulanır. Native rapor eylemi, bu yeni alanların yetkisini kendiliğinden sağlamaz.54 tanım ve SERVICE materyali exact kaynak schema/method/hash revizyonuna bağlanmadan gerçek çalışma etkinleşmez.

## paragraph:4345

Kabul senaryoları NOT_RUN: profile tanımı run olmadan yayımlanabilir; policy döngüsü yoktur. Child/descriptor kimlikleri retryda değişmez; parent-child hash döngüsü oluşmaz. Gizli vessel listesi daraltılmaz; empty company no-members gerektirir. Farklı metric/company/pair409; yanlış descriptor kind422; eksik source/fence503. DST ayrı sınırları ve28/31ayları doğru çözülür; leap TZif reddedilir. Bozuk/çok büyük arşiv allocation sınırında kesilir. Config değişimi olumlu kullanımı yeniden doğrulatır ama eski run/hash/receipt değişmez. Yazar kendi parser veya profilini bağımsız inceleyen sayılmaz. Runtime ve actual source fixture testleri yürütülmeden PASS üretilemez.

## paragraph:4346

Parent rapor için kapalı Node unionı: {t:NULL}|{t:BOOL,v:boolean}|{t:STRING,v:string}|{t:NUMBER,negative:boolean,coefficient:string,exponent:string}|{t:ARRAY,items:Node[]}|{t:OBJECT,entries:{key:string,value:Node}[]}. Her dalın alanları zorunludur ve ek alan yoktur. Numeric1 ile string "1", null ile string "null" farklı node türleridir. ARRAY sırası korunur. OBJECT keyleri duplicate kontrolünden sonra RFC8785 UTF16 code-unit sırasıyla sıralanır. String/key değerlerinde eşleşmemiş surrogate veya Unicode scalar olmayan veri JCS öncesinde reddedilir; replacement character ile sessiz düzeltme yapılmaz. Native NFC kuralı dışında trim, locale veya case dönüşümü yoktur.

## paragraph:4347

NUMBER dönüşümü IEEE parse öncesinde JSON sayı lexemesini kayıpsız okur. Gramer -?(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\.[0-9]+)?([eE][+-]?[0-9]+)? olur; native alan doğrulaması exponent, kesir veya negatif sıfırı ayrıca yasaklayabilir. Integer ve fraction digitleri birleştirilir; exponent=lexicalExponent−fractionDigitCount. Baştaki sıfırlar çıkarılır; nonzero coefficient sonundaki sıfırlar çıkarılırken exponent arttırılır. Coefficient yalnız unsigned digit dizisi, negative yalnız nonzero negatif değerde true, exponent canonical işaretli ondalık stringdir; artı işareti, leading zero veya -0 taşımaz. Native kuralın izin verdiği sıfır {negative:false,coefficient:"0",exponent:"0"} olur. Native yasak -0 önce reddedilir, normalizasyonla kabul edilmez.

## paragraph:4348

1,1.0,1e0 native doğrulamadan geçiyorsa aynı numeric node olur;1.01 coefficient="101",exponent="-2" olarak exact korunur.9223372036854775807 ve9007199254740993 değerleri binary floata çevrilmez. Node JSON içindeki sayı payloadları stringdir; unsafe numeric primitive kalmaz. Parent digest lowerhex(SHA256(UTF8(RFC8785JCS({codec:SI_REPORT_TYPED_TREE_HASH_V2,body:Node(validatedCompleteReportDraftSiV2)})))) olur. İşlem, adı version olan alanlara göre tahmin değil, kapalı ve açıkça kayıtlı tam ağaç dönüşümüdür.

## paragraph:4349

Native body/list/string/depth sınırları önce uygulanır. YeniV2 codec ek güvenlik sınırları: raw UTF8body16MiB, depth64, toplam1000000node, toplam string/key UTF8byte16MiB; sayı lexeme2048byte, significant coefficient1024digit, normalized base10 exponent mutlak değeri10000. Streaming doğrulama ve allocation bütçesi IEEE parse veya kontrolsüz recursive construction öncesinde sınırlandırılır. Bu sınırlar native int64 ve Decimal boyutlarını kayıpsız kapsar; aşım422 CANONICAL_ENCODING_UNSUPPORTED olur, yuvarlama/kırpma yoktur. Malformed JSON/duplicate key400, eksik accepted codec503 ve etki yoktur; eski codece fallback yapılmaz.

## paragraph:4350

Kayıpsız parent codec tasarım fixtureları: int64 maksimum ve53bitüstü farklı iki integer; exact1.01; kabul edilen1/1.0 eşitliği; numeric1/string1 ayrımı; object key sırasından bağımsızlık; array sıra duyarlılığı; duplicate key/NaN/exponent taşması reddi; native izinli signedzero; nonBMP key sırası. Bu salt codec prototip kontrolleri, native ReportDraft validatorı, deployment kabulü veya uygulama runtime testleri yerine geçmez. Kaynak/business testleri ve kaynak limitlerinin production parserda doğrulanması ayrıca gereklidir.

## paragraph:4351

CFG3 parametre seçimi için bağımsız kaynak adapteri

## paragraph:4352

Bu kaynak sözleşmesi yalnız 54.01 tüketicisinin si.population.profile_ref ve si.period.profile_ref anahtarlarını yönetir. SiPublishedProfileReadV1 tüketici64 portu veya system.reporting.si_profile.read hakkı burada kullanılamaz. Bu yeni kontrollü iç adapter kabulü public endpoint ya da canlı grant değildir.

## paragraph:4353

SiProfileCfg3TargetV1 requestClosed: {definitionRevisionRef:Native54RevisionRef,key:si.population.profile_ref|si.period.profile_ref,value:Native54RevisionRef|null,tenantId:UUID,companyId:UUID,selectors:{metricDefinitionId:UUID},effectiveAt:Instant,knownAt:Instant}; all fields mandatory, extra forbidden. key must match actual pinned definition. Request is internal server material; original human identity, authorization epoch, owner service admission and CFG3 phase supplied by native trusted call context, not request fields.

## paragraph:4354

SiProfileCfg3TargetV1 resultClosed: {state:RESOLVED,descriptor:SiProfileTargetDescriptorV1,child:SiPopulationProfileRevisionV1|SiPeriodProfileRevisionV1,parentState:PUBLISHED|DEPRECATED|WITHDRAWN,sourceFenceHash:Hash,validUntil:Instant}|{state:UNCONFIGURED|CONFLICT|UNKNOWN}; RESOLVED child kind must equal key/descriptor kind; every field mandatory, no extras; other branches expose no source identities. Result remains private. RESOLVED is identity resolution, not VALID publication or legal/safety decision.

## paragraph:4355

SiProfileCfg3TargetV1 producer: Source55 exact immutable descriptor repository lookup; nativeChildRef resolves actual typed child and parent publication index in the same tenant/company/metric. Verify descriptor own hash, full native child hash/version, immutable index and all current contribution permissions. Null value -> UNCONFIGURED; contradictory identity/company/metric -> CONFLICT; unavailable source -> UNKNOWN or native503 before trustworthy fence. Denial remains native403/404, never UNCONFIGURED/UNKNOWN fallback. New source method withReadFence invokes synchronous typed callback under shared55 source/current-human/authority fence through native54 validation/publication, transport result null. No exported bearer token, no independent check-then-write.

## paragraph:4356

SiProfileCfg3TargetV1 authority: Separate controlled55 source adapter binding for consumer54, exact two keys, purpose CFG3_PARAMETER_VALIDATION, schema/codec and methods. This does NOT reuse consumer64-only SiPublishedProfileReadV1 or its system.reporting.si_profile.read SERVICE grant. Native54 parameter action/scope plus current55 report/profile/metric/company/source field rights are AND. The source implementation may use the same private repository, not a cross-consumer service grant. No public endpoint or blanket human access is created.

## paragraph:4357

SiProfileCfg3TargetV1 publicationStage: Individual CFG3 publisher requires own target parent PUBLISHED and technical/full impact validity. DEPRECATED/WITHDRAWN source cannot newly publish selection. No current counterpart prerequisite. The source64 pair resolver remains separate and requires same parent/company/metric at positive commit; mixed pair PAIR_NOT_READY with no fallback. value:null→UNCONFIGURED only read/resolution; canonical non-null REVISION_REF publication rejects null, never VALID. RESOLVED identity with DEPRECATED/WITHDRAWN does not imply VALID; candidate publication requires parent PUBLISHED.

## paragraph:4358

SiProfileCfg3TargetV1 hashBoundary: Descriptor canonical54 ref points descriptor own digest; child native55 RevisionRef/hash/version retained unchanged. Callback output must not create new implicit hash codec. sourceFenceHash comes actual registered owner fence and remains private; its mere presence is not portable permission.

## paragraph:4359

Kaynak validation contract kimlikleri yalnız si.population.profile_ref.validate ve si.period.profile_ref.validate olur; POPULATION ve PERIOD ayrımı kendi anahtarıyla sabittir. Impact contract kimlikleri yalnız si.population.profile_ref.impact ve si.period.profile_ref.impact olur.

## paragraph:4360

Validation input: Native closed {definitionRevisionRef:RevisionRef,candidateValue:exact key schema,scopeContext:exact tenant/company/vessel,applicability:exact closed per-key selectors,effectiveAt:UTC,knownAt:UTC,sourceRevisionRefs:RevisionRef[1..100],authorizationEpoch:positive int64 string}. For this key candidateValue is canonical54 SiProfileTargetDescriptorV1 ref; scopeContext exact COMPANY+tenant; applicability only {metricDefinitionId:UUID}. Original actor comes from authenticated call context, never candidate body. SourceRevisionRefs are actual admitted owner contract/descriptor evidence refs with native codec; no fabricated UUIDs.

## paragraph:4361

Validation output: Native closed {status:VALID|INVALID|UNKNOWN,errors:{path:public schema path,code:registered safe code}[0..100],sourceFenceHash:SHA256,validUntil:UTC}. VALID only binding validity. Every returned fence must be actual registered owner fence; source/authority unavailable before such fence exists returns native503, never a fabricated hash or VALID. UNKNOWN cannot permit publication; errors never disclose private source values. Fence held through exact CFG3 publication, not merely GET then write.

## paragraph:4362

Validation kontrolleri: Kendi descriptor türü/kimliği/hashı, native child/parent bağı, current tenant/company/metric ve bütün kaynak katkı hakları aynı fence içinde doğrulanır. Candidate null kabul edilmez; olumlu publication yalnız PUBLISHED parent ister. Counterpart anahtarın bulunması veya aynı parentı seçmesi bireysel yayının önkoşulu değildir. Rapor/profil yayını,59 üyelik seçimi,64 policy mutationı veya hukukî/emniyet uygunluğu bu adapterin etkisi olamaz.

## paragraph:4363

Impact input: Native CFG3 closed {definitionRevisionId,oldResolvedSnapshot,newDraftHash,scope,selectors,effectiveFrom,dependencyVector}; exact proposed key revision and COMPANY/metric selector, actor/current rights from trusted context.

## paragraph:4364

Impact population: Complete authoritative dependency index for this key/company/metric/effective window: affected54 selections,64 policy revisions/current assessments and pending positive actions,55 plans/jobs with retained profile bindings. Enumerate all references to old/new descriptor and source children, including not visible to actor; protected details remain with owner. Missing consumer watermark or incomplete dependency enumeration is UNKNOWN/PARTIAL and blocks publication, not a filtered complete result.

## paragraph:4365

Impact effects: Current dependent positive caches REVOKE_POSITIVE_CACHE; affected current64 assessments REVALIDATE_CURRENT/REVIEW_REQUIRED through existing owner event flow. Immutable history PIN_EXISTING; new binding NEW_ONLY after current pair resolves. Separate two-key publication is allowed and can temporarily block positive use PAIR_NOT_READY; no invented atomic multi-key mutation. Actual callbacks do not rewrite source child or policy body.

## paragraph:4366

Impact output: Native closed {status,affectedSetHash,authorizedCount,blockingReasons,irreversibleEffects,consumerActions[],requiredApprovals[],sourceWatermarks,validUntil}; original native field types/limits apply. Only actual complete set hash/proof, hidden counts SUPPRESSED with owner review receipt. Any unresolved required dependency blocks publish. Dry-run no mutation; approved publication uses native config epoch/outbox, consumers deduplicate events and revalidate before next positive use.

## paragraph:4367

İki anahtarın ilk kurulumu veya sıralı değişimi sırasında eksik/karma pair için PAIR_NOT_READY olumlu kullanımı kapatır; eski değere fallback yoktur. Same-parent/company/metric şartı yalnız tutarlı pair çözümünde ve tüketici olumlu commitinde uygulanır. Bağımsız CFG3 review/publish tam etki incelemesiyle her anahtarda çalışır; native olmayan atomik multi-key endpoint icat edilmez. Tarihsel body/hash/receipt değişmez.

## paragraph:4368

Tasarlanmış kontroller NOT_RUN: Initial population key can publish with no period selection; positive64 pair remains PAIR_NOT_READY. Changing first key to new parent may publish after complete impact; old/new mixed pair blocks positive use until second key matches. 54 cannot invoke consumer64-only profile source method; wrong consumer/purpose rejects. Wrong descriptor kind/company/metric conflicts; unreadable child retains403/404. Denied consumer dependency detail is not filtered from impact completeness; protected count SUPPRESSED. Source revoked between resolution and publish aborts; no stale VALID reuse. Tam bağımlılık indeksinin uygulanması, source-service/şema/alan kaydının gerçek kabulü ve production eşzamanlılık testleri ayrı teslim koşuludur; bu analiz bunların yürütüldüğünü iddia etmez.

## paragraph:4369

Emniyet profili kaynak servisinin kontrollü kabulü

## paragraph:4370

Bu bölüm, yayımlanmış nüfus ve dönem profillerinin 64.01 emniyet tüketicisine okunması için servis kimliği, kaynak bağı, alan yetkisi, yaşam döngüsü ve bağımsız inceleme koşullarını kesinleştirir. Kaynak yöntem SiPublishedProfileReadV1.withReadFence olur. Asıl kullanıcının güncel rapor ve kaynak alan hakları, servis kimliği ve tüketicinin kendi işlem yetkisi birlikte zorunludur. Kaynak servisi rapor çalıştırma, filo seçimi, insan adına genel işlem veya hukuki uygunluk yetkisi vermez.

## paragraph:4371

Merkezi kayıt için aşağıdaki materyal gövdeleri kaynak sahibi 55.01 tarafından kabul edilen kapalı sözleşmelerdir; 54.02 bunları kayıt üretiminde aynı anlamla taşır. R gösterimi tahsis edilmiş kimlik değildir. Gerçek şema, kaynak türü, derlenmiş doğrulayıcı, inceleme makbuzu ve iş yükü kabulü tamamlanmadan servis çağrısı etkinleşmez. Bu metin canlı kayıt oluşturulduğu veya uygulama testlerinin çalıştırıldığı iddiası değildir.

## paragraph:4382

hashContract: New SI_PROFILE_DEPLOYMENT_HASH_V1. Identity/binding body preimage is {codec:SI_PROFILE_DEPLOYMENT_HASH_V1,recordType:IDENTITY|BINDING,body:<entire closed record excluding only top-level reviewReceiptRef and state>}. Before RFC8785JCS, exact /body/version positive-int64 wire numeric lexeme converts to its canonical base10 decimal string (no sign, leading zero, exponent, fraction; value1..9223372036854775807). No other numeric coercions. SHA256 UTF8JCS gives lowercase64hex. id/revisionId/version included, allocated before hash. Review preimage {codec:SI_PROFILE_DEPLOYMENT_REVIEW_HASH_V1,body:<whole closed review>}, exact /body/targetVersion converted same way; no other excluded review field. Native55 RevisionRef.hash and child hashes are stored source strings, not rehashed/cast by this codec. Source contracts -> identitybodyhash -> detachedidentityreview -> bindingbodyhash -> detachedbindingreview. No reverse pointers. Mutable authoritative lifecycle state checked separately at invocation/fence commit, old reviewed hash never overrides revoke/expiry.

errors: Authenticate/authorize and native nondisclosure first; forbidden or unknown protected source403/404. UNAVAILABLE only native unresolved historical/absent source case, never denied permission or fabricated empty fleet. Source revoked before callback end aborts/retries through native stable command reconciliation, never commits old grant.

lifecycle: PUBLISHED required for new profile selection. WITHDRAWN closes all positive use and dependent caches immediately. DEPRECATED prohibits new bindings; exact already-pinned continuation only if source64 accepted operation explicitly supplies its native continuation rule and passes it. If no explicit continuation contract, deny positive use; no general historic exemption. Read may show only authorized native current parentState without implying usable profile.

lifecycleCustody.shape: NEW SiProfileDeploymentLifecycleV1={targetType:IDENTITY|BINDING,targetId:UUID,targetRevisionId:UUID,targetMaterialVersion:positiveInt64,targetBodyHash:Hash64,state:DRAFT|REVIEWED|ACTIVE|REVOKED|EXPIRED,stateVersion:positiveInt64,reviewReceiptRef:IAMRef|null,changedAt:Instant,changeCommandId:UUID}; allrequired/noextras. One authoritative mutable row per immutable targettuple. stateVersion starts1 only with actual newtarget, increments1 underCAS, int64overflow503/noeffect. TargetBodyHash unchanged by lifecycle.

## paragraph:4435

lifecycleCustody.immutableBoundary: Identity/binding material id/revision/version+hashpreimage body immutable. State/reviewReceiptRef in wire are joined current lifecycle projection, not immutablebodyhash. Canonical54 targetref points new SiProfileServiceIdentityRevision/SiProfileSourceBindingRevision body only. Workload/scope/validity/admission change requires newrevision/materialversion; no inplaceactiveedit.

## paragraph:4436

lifecycleCustody.producer: Protected55 deployment registrar creates material+initialDRAFT row atomically. Only current source55/security authorizeddeploymentactor, stable nativecommandidempotency, exactstateVersion/targethashCAS. GET cannotallocate. No synthetic legacy lifecycle. Statechangeappendonlyaudit/event uses actualchangeCommandId and serverchangedAt.

## paragraph:4437

lifecycleCustody.transitions: DRAFT->REVIEWED only independentAPPROVE exacttargetreceipt; rejectedreview creates event but cannotactivate. REVIEWED->ACTIVE after current acceptedsource/codec/workload/scope andvalidFrom<=now<validUntil; BINDING alsoexactidentityACTIVE+subset. DRAFT|REVIEWED|ACTIVE->REVOKED authorizedreducingtransition even ifother sourcesunavailable. now>=validUntil effectiveEXPIRED immediately regardlessworkerlag; workerCAS persistsit. REVOKED/EXPIRED terminal, newrevisionrequired. Invalidtransition409; sourceautherror503/deny.

## paragraph:4438

lifecycleCustody.invocation: Beforeoperation AND callbackcommit samefence verifies identity+binding exactmaterial/lifecycle tuple,stateVersion,state,reviewReceiptRef/hash, ACTIVE andvalidity pluscurrentissuer/workload/sourceconsumeracceptance. Revoke/expiry wins beforecommit aborts/noeffect; no historicalcachedgrant. Review authenticity/sourceacceptance checked, durable review does not inventshortTTL. Reviewer qualification atreview audit-bound; currentdeploymentoperator permission checked separately.

localPackageReview.shape: NEW SiProfilePackageReviewV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,packageKey:SI_PROFILE_SERVICE_LOCAL_V1,authorPersonId:UUID,authorPersonIds:UUID[1..1000],targetProvenanceRefs:IAMRef[17],reviewScopeHash:Hash64,targetRefs:IAMRef[17],targetSetHash:Hash64,sourceApprovalRefs:IAMRef[2],reviews:RoleReview[3],decision:APPROVE|REJECT,recordedAt:Instant}; RoleReview={role:SOURCE55|CONSUMER64|SECURITY,reviewerPersonId:UUID,decision:APPROVE|REJECT,reviewedAt:Instant,authorityEvidenceRef:IAMRef,reviewEvidenceRef:IAMRef}. Exact17unique refs sortedcanonical(ownerDomain,objectType,objectId,revisionId,contentHash) tuple. Missing/extra/duplicate rejects. Reviewers inspectexactset+authorprovenance+sourceapprovals. NEW detached SiProfilePackageRoleEvidenceV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,packageKey:SI_PROFILE_SERVICE_LOCAL_V1,targetSetHash:Hash64,reviewScopeHash:Hash64,role:SOURCE55|CONSUMER64|SECURITY,reviewerPersonId:UUID,decision:APPROVE|REJECT,reviewedAt:Instant,authorityEvidenceRef:IAMRef}; allrequired/noextras,hashwholebodyafteridallocation. RoleReview exactlymatches itsevidence; sourceapprovalcannotstandinforlocalreview.

## paragraph:4443

localPackageReview.codec: Packagehash=SHA256 UTF8JCS({codec:SI_PROFILE_PACKAGE_REVIEW_HASH_V1,body:wholeclosedpackagebody}). MaterialapprovalRefs use preexisting sourcecontract approval, never thispackageRef. Thus materialbytes/refs -> detachedroleevidence -> package; nocycle. ## paragraph:4444

localPackageReview.publication: AtomicregistryCAS pins currentselectedpredecessor/catalogepoch, exact17refs/hashes,currentreview/source/type acceptance. Any mismatch409/503 publishesnone. Separate actualworkloadidentity+bindingACTIVE required; package alone no livegrant. Current trusted provenance/reviewScopeHash and all-author SoD rechecked atomically; materialwriter cannotreviewthroughdifferentpackagebuilder.

## paragraph:4445

localPackageReview.provenance: For each of17 immutabletargetrefs, registrar verifies trusted owner-generated immutable target provenance matching exacttargettuple/hash and actualbody/schema/sourceartifact authorPersonIds; not callerdeclared names. targetProvenanceRefs exact17 in same targetRefs order. authorPersonIds sortedunique union of everytargetauthor andpackage builder authorPersonId, 1..1000; overflow ormissing/unverifiableprovenance fails503/NOT_CONFIGURED, no truncatedunion. reviewScopeHash=SHA256 UTF8JCS({packageKey,targetSetHash,authorPersonIds,targetProvenanceRefs,sourceApprovalRefs}). RoleEvidence.reviewScopeHash and targetSetHash mustequal package; allreviewersdistinct andoutsidecompleteauthorunion. Anychangedauthor/provenance/approval invalidatesallthree reviews evenifmaterialrefsunchanged.

## paragraph:4446

Kabul senaryoları

## paragraph:4447

SI SERVICE 01: Yanlış tüketici, amaç, yöntem veya kayıtlı işlem revizyonu çağrısı reddedilir; genel servis anahtarı başka çağrıya yetki vermez.

## paragraph:4448

SI SERVICE 02: Kaynak çiftinin şirketi, metriği, parent revizyonu veya child türü uyuşmazsa 409; gizli kaynak 403/404 olur. Yetki reddi UNAVAILABLE veya boş filo sonucuna dönüştürülmez.

## paragraph:4449

SI SERVICE 03: Kaynak ya da servis yetkisi callback tamamlanmadan kaldırılırsa olumlu işlem uygulanmaz. Salt okuma yanıtında gizli referans, hash veya sayı sızdırılmaz.

## paragraph:4450

SI SERVICE 04: Servis kapsamı veya geçerlilik süresi değiştirilen malzeme yeni değişmez revizyon ve inceleme gerektirir. Eski onay veya önbellek terminal REVOKED veya EXPIRED durumunu açamaz.

## paragraph:4451

SI SERVICE 05: On yedi materyalden birinin yazarı üç paket inceleyicisinden biriyse kabul reddedilir. Eksik kaynak yazarlığı veya değişmiş inceleme kapsamı eski onayla yayımlanamaz.

## paragraph:4452

SI SERVICE 06: Native rapor refi, 54 canonical refi ve 64 refi arasında tahmini tip dönüşümü yapılmaz. Int64 üst sınırı kayıpsız hashlenir; durum değişikliği ayrı stateVersion CAS ile denetlenir.

## paragraph:4453

SI SERVICE 07: Nüfus profili okuması 55 rapor çalıştırması veya 59 seçim kaydı üretmez. 54 parametre doğrulama adapteri bu 64 tüketici iznini kullanamaz.

## paragraph:4454

SI SERVICE 08: Negatif inceleme ve emekliye ayırma işlemleri olumlu profil servisinin on dokuz tüketici kabulü üzerinden yetkilendirilmez; kendi azaltıcı geçiş kuralları korunur.

## paragraph:4455

Bu senaryolar geliştirici kabul testleridir; çalışma zamanı test sonucu değildir. Kaynak ve merkezi kayıt aynı exact şema ve materyal revizyonunu kabul etmeden etkinleştirme kapalı kalır.

## paragraph:4456

Şema üretimi ve bağımsız doğrulama

## paragraph:4457

boundary: NEW source-owned normative contract SI_PROFILE_SCHEMA_BUILD_V1. These are specified registry inputs, not assertions that artifacts, tests, signatures, reviewers or deployment already exist. All records closed/all fields required; IAMRef is exact canonical54 five-field immutable ref, not source55 RevisionRef. UUID/Hash64/Instant retain native definitions. Unknown or unverifiable dependencies block registration with NOT_CONFIGURED, never synthesize proof.

## paragraph:4458

artifact: SiProfileValidatorArtifactV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,sourceOwner:55.01,registrationRef:IAMRef,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,sourceBytesHash:Hash64,buildBytesHash:Hash64,sourceBytesLength:DecimalUnsignedString,buildBytesLength:DecimalUnsignedString,sourceObjectRef:IAMRef,buildObjectRef:IAMRef,buildRecipeRef:IAMRef,authorPersonIds:UUID[1..1000],buildAttestationRef:IAMRef,createdAt:Instant}. Length strings canonical 0|[1-9][0-9]*, <= signed-int64 max. Protected source build records actual retrieved bytes SHA256/length, immutable object refs and trusted build recipe. Author set sorted unique union of actual source/export/build authors, not caller assertions. Attestation is an already authorized build-evidence service record binding exact source/build bytes hashes, recipe, registration ref and author set; the service must verify its existing signed evidence format/authority. This contract does not create signature authority or accept an opaque unattested ref. Registration has no artifact ref, so no cycle.

## paragraph:4459

conformance: SiProfileSchemaConformanceV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,artifactRef:IAMRef,registrationRef:IAMRef,runnerAttestationRef:IAMRef,runAt:Instant,imports:VerifiedImport[4],entries:SchemaResult[6],result:PASS|FAIL}. VerifiedImport={slot:exact registration import slot,sourceTypeContractRef:IAMRef,typePath:Text200,contractHash:Hash64,compiledTypeHash:Hash64,typeExportRef:IAMRef,shapeBytesRef:IAMRef,shapeCodecRef:IAMRef,exportEvidenceRef:IAMRef}; SchemaResult={schemaKey:exact six descriptor keys,descriptorHash:Hash64,validatorExport:Text140,codecExport:Text140,testSuiteRef:IAMRef,testResultRef:IAMRef,result:PASS|FAIL}. Arrays complete unique exact sets, lexicographic slot/schemaKey order. Every import tuple equals registration; compiledTypeHash is digest of actual compiler-exported canonical source type shape under its controlled source codec, never text-name hash. Every entry equals registration and its actual descriptor; actual protected runner loads exact build bytes, resolves all four imports and executes the pinned test suites. Test evidence must contain positive/negative closed-shape, extra/missing/null/type/enum/overflow and codec deterministic fixtures appropriate to each schema plus native-import equivalence; result PASS iff all six evidence results PASS and all imports verified. Runner attestation uses existing trusted execution-evidence service verified format, pins artifact/registration/suites/result hashes; unverifiable authority or missing fixture evidence fails acceptance.

## paragraph:4460

review: SiProfileArtifactReviewV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,artifactRef:IAMRef,registrationRef:IAMRef,conformanceRef:IAMRef,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,reviewerPersonId:UUID,reviewerAuthorityRef:IAMRef,decision:APPROVE|REJECT,reviewedAt:Instant,reason:Text10..2000}. Source55-qualified independent reviewer outside entire artifact authorPersonIds; current reviewer authority verified by native authority service. All referenced hashes/owners and conformance PASS/current unrevoked dependencies checked. Source approval is preexisting source-contract approval, not this review. Review cannot overwrite artifact bytes or confer runtime SERVICE access.

## paragraph:4461

emittedMaterial: SiProfileGeneratedSchemaMaterialV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,sourceOwner:55.01,schemaKey:exact six descriptor keys,descriptorHash:Hash64,registrationRef:IAMRef,artifactRef:IAMRef,conformanceRef:IAMRef,artifactReviewRef:IAMRef,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,validatorExport:Text140,codecExport:Text140,sourceTypeImports:VerifiedImport[4]}. Protected registrar emits exactly one material per key only after approved independent artifact review; exports and descriptor hash equal registration and conformance. All four imports pinned to the exact same verified set even for schemas using only a subset. Registry SchemaRef is IAMRef owner54.01/objectType SchemaRevision/objectId=id/revisionId/contentHash=material hash. Schema implementation resolves exact artifact export; never executes descriptor prose as a validator. Material binds review but review does not bind future material refs. SourceOwner55.01 is producer provenance; canonical registry ownership is54.01, matching every R(54.01,SchemaRevision,key) lookup. No alternate55 schema namespace is emitted.

## paragraph:4462

hashAndDAG: For artifact, conformance, artifact review and generated material: SHA256(UTF8(JCS({codec:<SI_PROFILE_VALIDATOR_ARTIFACT_HASH_V1|SI_PROFILE_SCHEMA_CONFORMANCE_HASH_V1|SI_PROFILE_ARTIFACT_REVIEW_HASH_V1|SI_PROFILE_GENERATED_SCHEMA_HASH_V1>,body:whole closed record}))). All UUIDs allocated before hashing, no hash/ref of self in body; decimal lengths are strings. Registration hash uses SI_PROFILE_SCHEMA_REGISTRATION_HASH_V1 with its complete closed body and same wrapper; descriptor hashes retain SHA256 UTF8JCS(descriptor). Existing evidence service object hashes/codecs are imported, not redefined. Order: accepted source contracts/type exports + source approval -> registration -> actual source/build objects and attestation -> artifact -> test runner evidence -> conformance -> detached artifact review -> six schema materials -> 17-target package provenance/review -> atomic registry publication. Conformance/runner evidence never references future conformance or review; review never references future material/package.

## paragraph:4463

admissionAndErrors: At atomic publication, exact owner/type/id/revision/hash resolve each ref; registration/import tuples and six schema set equal, artifact bytes hashes and lengths verified, trusted signatures/current revocation state valid, runner result PASS, independent author-union exclusion holds. All six emitted refs must share registration/artifact/conformance/review/sourceApproval/import pins. Invalid closed input 422; hidden/unreadable dependency follows native non-disclosure 404; authority denial 403; stale registry/review dependency 409; absent compiler/export/trust evidence NOT_CONFIGURED/503. No partial six-schema publication. Retirement/revocation of accepted dependency disables positive new use under native registry lifecycle and does not rewrite immutable bodies. Existing trust/evidence services are mandatory external prerequisites, not fabricated new authority.

## paragraph:4464

referenceEnvelope: Protected registrar allocates registration id and revisionId before registration hashing. All new records are immutable after acceptance: canonical54 IAMRef uses ownerDomain55.01, objectType equal exact SiProfileSchemaRegistrationV1, SiProfileValidatorArtifactV1, SiProfileSchemaConformanceV1 or SiProfileArtifactReviewV1, objectId=record.id, revisionId=record.revisionId, contentHash=that record codec digest. GeneratedSchemaMaterial is the sole exception: ownerDomain54.01/objectType SchemaRevision as declared above. Registrar enforces unique owner/type/id/revision, stores exact body and digest together atomically, and resolves exact tuple plus current acceptance/revocation state. New revision allocates revisionId, never changes old body. Native source-object, build-evidence, authority and type-contract references retain their existing registered owners/types and must be verified against their real service; these are not assigned invented source55 types.

## paragraph:4465

SI SCHEMA 01: Altı şemadan birinin test sonucu eksik veya FAIL ise altı şemanın hiçbiri yayımlanmaz. Dört dış türün isim eşitliği yeterli değildir; gerçek kontrollü tür hashleri doğrulanır.

## paragraph:4466

SI SCHEMA 02: Kaynak veya derleme bayt hashinin değişmesi, eski derleyici kanıtı ya da bağımsız inceleme ile kabul edilemez. Yazar birleşimindeki bir kişinin incelemesi reddedilir.

## paragraph:4467

SI SCHEMA 03: Kayıt, artifact, conformance, review ve üretilen materyal referansları ayrı kesin tür ve hashlerle çözülür. Gelecekte üretilecek materyal referansı önceki onayın hashine eklenmez; döngü oluşturulmaz.

## paragraph:4468

importExportCustody: For each registration Import, contractHash MUST equal sourceTypeContractRef.contentHash. VerifiedImport must carry that exact tuple unchanged. typeExportRef pins an actual already accepted immutable source-owned type-export descriptor for exactly sourceTypeContractRef/typePath; its registered closed descriptor must bind the same shapeBytesRef, shapeCodecRef, compiledTypeHash and exporter identity. shapeBytesRef resolves immutable canonical exported type-shape bytes; shapeCodecRef resolves exact approved source codec revision defining those bytes, not an unnamed compiler default. SHA256 of retrieved canonical shape bytes MUST equal compiledTypeHash; exporter evidence verifies those bytes were produced by that codec from the pinned source type. exportEvidenceRef is the actual trusted export/build service evidence pinning descriptor, source contract, typePath, shape bytes hash and codec; authenticated producer/authority/current revocation verified. Unsupported source descriptor/evidence schema, missing exporter evidence or absent approved codec is NOT_CONFIGURED and blocks all six registrations. This imports actual registered source export/evidence contracts, not invented new signer authority or arbitrary blob proof. The runner verifies four exact exported type shapes against validator build imports, not name/hash strings alone. Source exports and evidence exist before registration/artifact/conformance; no reference to future conformance or generated material.

## paragraph:4469

Genel rapor tanımı V3: kapalı işlem, veri ve yetki sözleşmesi

## paragraph:4470

Bu bölüm genel rapor tanımı oluşturma, düzenleme ve ayrıntı okuma için açık REPORT_DEFINITION_V3 sürümünü tanımlar. Önceki ReportDraftV1, SI_PROFILE_BUNDLE_V2 ve SI_PROFILE_SCHEMA_BUILD_V1 sözleşmeleri değiştirilmez. Yeni tasarım ve kabul koşulları yazılımın kurulduğu, gerçek yetki kayıtlarının yayımlandığı veya cihaz testlerinin geçtiği anlamına gelmez. Gerçek kaynak, bağımsız inceleme, derleme ve uygulama kanıtları olmadan etkinleştirme kapalıdır.

Presentationmetadata view fromsameversion.presentation only; nooutsidebody input. Partialcompound AST,measuredefs,parameterdefinitions,filters,sort,metricbindings andSI canonicalrefs are all-or-omit atminimumclosedtypedvalue andfullcontributor scope. IndividualSIleafread onlyviaexplicitprofileprojection schema, notpartialcanonicalSiProfileBundleInput placed indefinition. WholecanonicalReportDefinitionbody requiredforEDITABLE; unchangedhiddenfields neverdeletedbypartialprojection.

## paragraph:4569

availableActionsBoundary

## paragraph:4570

Required availableActions list is [] whennoauthorizeddisclosableaction or ownREADfielddenied; neveromitted, nohiddenactioncount/reason. Currentactoronly andnoSERVICEkeys. Publication optionalleaves have separate9fieldkeys underparentpublicationREAD; omitnoauthorizedemptypublication, do notexposeallvia parentgrant.

## paragraph:4571

audienceAcceptance

## paragraph:4572

Active PINNED grant for another version does not mark selected version SHARED.

## paragraph:4573

CURRENT_APPROVED native pointer unknown or source unavailable omits audience, never UNSHARED.

## paragraph:4574

Filtered native share GET returns zero while protected full population has grants: public filtered zero is not accepted as complete source.

## paragraph:4575

A valid share does not grant RUN/source/export rights; native54 evaluator still controls every operation.

## paragraph:4576

Revocation before serialization prevents stale SHARED presentation; summary itself is not authority.

## paragraph:4577

validationCodeProjection

## paragraph:4578

New V3 safe issue codes are exactly REQUIRED_FIELD, FIELD_TYPE, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, SOURCE_NOT_ACCEPTED, DEPENDENCY_CHANGED, UNIT_MISMATCH, EXPRESSION_CYCLE, FILTER_INVALID, PARAMETER_INVALID, PRESENTATION_INVALID, LIMIT_EXCEEDED, VALIDATION_FAILED. Native validator typed failure maps to corresponding exact category; any other actual failure maps VALIDATION_FAILED, never success. messageKey must equal reporting.validation.v3.<code> with exact lowercase code; text localized from registered tr/en messages, never arbitrary raw error. path is empty root string or one exact /definition/<field> from the19 definitionFields names, never arbitrary source paths. Hidden contributor requires omitted entire validation property under source rights. Existing stored failure severity retained ERROR|WARNING; unknown severity maps ERROR. VALID requires no ERROR and complete current semantic proof; INCOMPLETE/INVALID cannot become VALID from filtered issues. These are new successor presentation codes, not renaming native historical validator receipts.

## paragraph:4579

availableActionsFinite

## paragraph:4580

Exact record-centric actions in this projection are reporting.report.read/update/validate/copy/favorite/submit_review/approve/reject/publish/new_version/deprecate/withdraw/archive from native T40/T41. Unique array max13, sorted lexical. Each requires exact currently registered native route/action and actual selected root/state/prerequisites. Create, run, export, sharing and unrelated SERVICE operations remain their own native control contexts, not invented selected-report actions. Missing approved action mapping yields no action, never prefix-derived capability.

## paragraph:4581

createPresencePolicy

## paragraph:4582

source: Native55 T33 defaults and T39 incomplete draft; successor V3 finite serialization choices explicitly versioned, not inferred legacy behavior.

## paragraph:4583

required: ["name","purpose","category_id"]

## paragraph:4584

normalization: Create and PATCH name: trim leading/trailing Unicode whitespace, then validate3..120 Unicode codepoints; retain internal whitespace, no case folding. Persist normalized value before body hash; input shape validation must run after this explicit field normalization. No analogous trimming of arbitrary literal/parameter values.

## paragraph:4585

omittedCreate: {"description":"STORE empty string","visualization":"STORE TABLE","locale":"STORE tr-TR","timezone":"STORE actual tenant IANA zone captured at create transaction, validated against pinned tzdb. Missing/unreadable/invalid tenant zone fails SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE; no local/browser fallback.","groups":"STORE []","measures":"STORE []","parameter_definitions":"STORE []","sort":"STORE []","dataset_id":"ABSENT incomplete DRAFT","dataset_version_id":"ABSENT incomplete DRAFT","selected_fields":"ABSENT incomplete DRAFT; do not store illegal []","filter_ast":"ABSENT","metricEvidenceBindings":"ABSENT","siProfileBundle":"ABSENT","presentation":"ABSENT","output_formats":"If dataset_id/dataset_version_id is intentionally absent in minimal DRAFT, STORE ABSENT; no derived classification or dataset policy lookup is required and this is not SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE. Once full selected source exists, read actual report classification and current export policy in same create fence: ordered [CSV,XLSX,PDF] intersection; nonempty STORE, empty or Restricted default disabled => ABSENT. Configured source service outage/unreadable policy => SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE. Later dataset assignment does not silently apply defaults on PATCH; explicit output_formats edit and policy validation required."}

## paragraph:4586

explicitValues: Supplied values must satisfy schema and native semantic/source authorization. Explicit output_formats must be permitted current policy subset; prohibited request fails, never silently broadens. Explicit timezone validates actual IANA/tzdb. Required fields cannot be absent/null. No null defaults except defined nullable fields. Explicit output_formats without complete selected dataset/version rejects422 SOURCE_REQUIRED_FOR_OUTPUT_FORMATS; caller may omit for minimal DRAFT. No export authority is inferred from absence.

## paragraph:4587

patch: Every omitted field KEEP. No create default reapplied on PATCH. Present value replaces only authorized full field, with source/default-free validation; empty arrays allowed only field cardinality permits; null only filter_ast/siProfileBundle.

## paragraph:4588

hash: Materialized defaults included exactly once in canonical body before hashing. No hidden current tenant defaults re-evaluated on subsequent read or PATCH; original creation source provenance retained server-side. Changing tenant configuration cannot rewrite historical body.

## paragraph:4589

expressionCompileClosure

## paragraph:4590

designVersion: REPORT_DEFINITION_V3 new expression semantics, not inferred legacy evaluator behavior

## paragraph:4591

typeRules: Every EXPRESSION measure result must be INTEGER or DECIMAL, declared unit must equal derived registered unit, aggregation tag cannot claim additive for nonadditive derived result. STRING/BOOLEAN/DATE/INSTANT/REFERENCE/CODE expression nodes may only participate in explicitly permitted comparison/COUNT/MIN/MAX/DATE_DIFF/CASE contexts. No list-to-scalar conversion. ADD/SUBTRACT require same numeric type/unit/grain, no implicit cast. MULTIPLY/DIVIDE require registered resulting dimensional unit and compatible grain; otherwise422. COUNT counts each eligible known row/value in selected authorized population without deduplication; COUNT_DISTINCT deduplicates by exact canonical typed value/key under source type identity. No unfiltered global count shortcut. NULL/UNKNOWN/NOT_APPLICABLE are tracked by native quality lineage and never counted as known data or silently hidden as complete population.

## paragraph:4592

aggregateRules: Nested aggregate of GROUP output is rejected; reaggregation requires a separately registered metric/source contract, never average-of-averages. SUM rejects identifiers and nonadditive rate/ratio measures. WEIGHTED_MEAN operands numeric same row grain, nonnegative weights; output NON_ADDITIVE, source composable numerator/denominator retained for fleet combination. ADD/SUBTRACT additive result requires both operands additive and source-domain additivity; CASE result additive only if every possible then/else contributor is additive and source-grain rules permit. Never choose the left or else flag alone. Nonadditive lineage remains through nesting.

## paragraph:4593

graphBudget: Measure ids unique; every reference same report revision, full direct/transitive source rights checked. Detect cycles before expansion. Across all compiled report expression roots, count expanded references/nodes with no memo bypass; maximum100 evaluated expression nodes and depth10 including measure-reference edges. Reject expansion before exceeding bound. Max20 stored measures remains. Bounded newV3 acceptance rule, not silently applied to legacy expression formats.

## paragraph:4594

qualityRules: Compile-time constant divisor0 rejected422. At run dynamic divisor0=>UNKNOWN/DIVIDE_BY_ZERO, never0. Dynamic negative weight=>UNKNOWN/INVALID_WEIGHT; totalweight0=>UNKNOWN/ZERO_TOTAL_WEIGHT. Unknown or NA inputs follow source quality lineage, not implicitzero. CASE evaluates ordered branches, unknown earlier branch that could change chosen result makes UNKNOWN; equal independently proven candidate outputs may collapse only under registered proof policy, otherwise UNKNOWN. State predicates explicit, no empty or missing value cast.

## paragraph:4595

calendarRules: DATE_DIFF INSTANT only; elapsed UTC units and pinned-tzdb CALENDAR_DAYS distinct. Negative intervals permitted only by selected source field semantic domain, otherwise invalid. Actual IANA/codec/conversion resolution cannot be established by synthetic unit tests.

## paragraph:4596

fixtureBoundary: 37 local synthetic compile checks cover selected DAG/type/unit/grain/filter errors. Not all runtime numerical/calendar/source privacy semantics, not deployed product validation.

## paragraph:4597

storedSchemaLifecycleDispatch

## paragraph:4598

All existing validate/submit-review/approve/reject/publish/new-version/deprecate/withdraw/archive/copy/run handlers resolve immutable stored contentSchemaVersion, never guess from request header, present field names or latestserverdefault. REPORT_DEFINITION_V3 selects the exact accepted five-schema family/bodycodec and V3 semantic compiler plus every native domain/approval/SoD/source fence. Lifecycle request/receipt/action identities remain existing native types; no new authority. UNKNOWN storedversion or unavailable approved V3 compiler returns503 REPORT_SCHEMA_NOT_CONFIGURED, no legacyvalidator fallback. Validation receipts/approval artifacts bind exact reportId/versionId/bodyhash/bodycodec/compiler build and all pinned source dependencies; changed source or compiler invalidates relevant validation under existing lifecycle, not silent review reuse. Copy/new-version from V3 preserves exact typed fields into separately authorized new DRAFT, newbodyhash/review required, no sharing/approval/grant copies; legacy-toV3 remains explicit typed conversion only. Run uses published selected immutableV3 body and exact validated compiler output; current caller source/action/field guards still apply and arbitrary SQL/usercode prohibited. Without actual V3-enabled lifecycle/run handler enforcement evidence, create/read design can exist disabled but wholeV3product activation is prohibited.

## paragraph:4709

Beş kontrollü şema kökü

## paragraph:4710

Her şemaBody tam olarak {$schema:"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",$id:<aşağıdaki $id>,$ref:<schemaRoot>,$defs:<yukarıdaki tam sözlük>} nesnesidir. Şema sözlüğü atlanmaz veya köke göre daraltılmaz. schemaBytesHash, GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_SOURCE_JSON_V1 ile bu nesnenin tam baytlarından hesaplanır.

family

## paragraph:4717

GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_BUILD_V3

## paragraph:4718

isolation

## paragraph:4719

Separate build from SI_PROFILE_SCHEMA_BUILD_V1. Existing six-schema/four-import source service family unchanged. This general family is exactly five entries and three imports; it grants no read/write/runtime authority.

## paragraph:4720

imports

## paragraph:4721

## paragraph:4724

requiredCustody

## paragraph:4725

importExportCustody: For each registration Import, contractHash MUST equal sourceTypeContractRef.contentHash. VerifiedImport must carry that exact tuple unchanged. typeExportRef pins an actual already accepted immutable source-owned type-export descriptor for exactly sourceTypeContractRef/typePath; its registered closed descriptor must bind the same shapeBytesRef, shapeCodecRef, compiledTypeHash and exporter identity. shapeBytesRef resolves immutable canonical exported type-shape bytes; shapeCodecRef resolves exact approved source codec revision defining those bytes, not an unnamed compiler default. SHA256 of retrieved canonical shape bytes MUST equal compiledTypeHash; exporter evidence verifies those bytes were produced by that codec from the pinned source type. exportEvidenceRef is the actual trusted export/build service evidence pinning descriptor, source contract, typePath, shape bytes hash and codec; authenticated producer/authority/current revocation verified. Unsupported source descriptor/evidence schema, missing exporter evidence or absent approved codec is NOT_CONFIGURED and blocks all five registrations. This imports actual registered source export/evidence contracts, not invented new signer authority or arbitrary blob proof. The runner verifies three exact exported type shapes against validator build imports, not name/hash strings alone. Source exports and evidence exist before registration/artifact/conformance; no reference to future conformance or generated material.

## paragraph:4726

closedRecords

## paragraph:4727

artifact: GeneralReportValidatorArtifactV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,sourceOwner:55.01,registrationRef:IAMRef,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,sourceBytesHash:Hash64,buildBytesHash:Hash64,sourceBytesLength:DecimalUnsignedString,buildBytesLength:DecimalUnsignedString,sourceObjectRef:IAMRef,buildObjectRef:IAMRef,buildRecipeRef:IAMRef,authorPersonIds:UUID[1..1000],buildAttestationRef:IAMRef,createdAt:Instant}. Length strings canonical 0|[1-9][0-9]*, <= signed-int64 max. Protected source build records actual retrieved bytes SHA256/length, immutable object refs and trusted build recipe. Author set sorted unique union of actual source/export/build authors, not caller assertions. Attestation is an already authorized build-evidence service record binding exact source/build bytes hashes, recipe, registration ref and author set; the service must verify its existing signed evidence format/authority. This contract does not create signature authority or accept an opaque unattested ref. Registration has no artifact ref, so no cycle.

## paragraph:4728

conformance: GeneralReportSchemaConformanceV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,artifactRef:IAMRef,registrationRef:IAMRef,runnerAttestationRef:IAMRef,runAt:Instant,imports:VerifiedImport[3],entries:SchemaResult[5],result:PASS|FAIL}. VerifiedImport={slot:exact registration import slot,sourceTypeContractRef:IAMRef,typePath:Text200,contractHash:Hash64,compiledTypeHash:Hash64,typeExportRef:IAMRef,shapeBytesRef:IAMRef,shapeCodecRef:IAMRef,exportEvidenceRef:IAMRef}; SchemaResult={schemaKey:exact five descriptor keys,descriptorHash:Hash64,validatorExport:Text140,codecExport:Text140,testSuiteRef:IAMRef,testResultRef:IAMRef,result:PASS|FAIL}. Arrays complete unique exact sets, lexicographic slot/schemaKey order. Every import tuple equals registration; compiledTypeHash is digest of actual compiler-exported canonical source type shape under its controlled source codec, never text-name hash. Every entry equals registration and its actual descriptor; actual protected runner loads exact build bytes, resolves all three imports and executes the pinned test suites. Test evidence must contain positive/negative closed-shape, extra/missing/null/type/enum/overflow and codec deterministic fixtures appropriate to each schema plus native-import equivalence; result PASS iff all five evidence results PASS and all imports verified. Runner attestation uses existing trusted execution-evidence service verified format, pins artifact/registration/suites/result hashes; unverifiable authority or missing fixture evidence fails acceptance.

## paragraph:4729

review: GeneralReportArtifactReviewV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,artifactRef:IAMRef,registrationRef:IAMRef,conformanceRef:IAMRef,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,reviewerPersonId:UUID,reviewerAuthorityRef:IAMRef,decision:APPROVE|REJECT,reviewedAt:Instant,reason:Text10..2000}. Source55-qualified independent reviewer outside entire artifact authorPersonIds; current reviewer authority verified by native authority service. All referenced hashes/owners and conformance PASS/current unrevoked dependencies checked. Source approval is preexisting source-contract approval, not this review. Review cannot overwrite artifact bytes or confer runtime SERVICE access.

## paragraph:4730

emittedMaterial: GeneralReportGeneratedSchemaMaterialV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,sourceOwner:55.01,schemaKey:exact five descriptor keys,descriptorHash:Hash64,registrationRef:IAMRef,artifactRef:IAMRef,conformanceRef:IAMRef,artifactReviewRef:IAMRef,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,validatorExport:Text140,codecExport:Text140,sourceTypeImports:VerifiedImport[3]}. Protected registrar emits exactly one material per key only after approved independent artifact review; exports and descriptor hash equal registration and conformance. All three imports pinned to the exact same verified set even for schemas using only a subset. Registry SchemaRef is IAMRef owner54.01/objectType SchemaRevision/objectId=id/revisionId/contentHash=material hash. Schema implementation resolves exact artifact export; never executes descriptor prose as a validator. Material binds review but review does not bind future material refs. SourceOwner55.01 is producer provenance; canonical registry ownership is54.01, matching every R(54.01,SchemaRevision,key) lookup. No alternate55 schema namespace is emitted.

## paragraph:4731

hashAndDAG: For artifact, conformance, artifact review and generated material: SHA256(UTF8(JCS({codec:<GENERAL_REPORT_VALIDATOR_ARTIFACT_HASH_V1|GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_CONFORMANCE_HASH_V1|GENERAL_REPORT_ARTIFACT_REVIEW_HASH_V1|GENERAL_REPORT_GENERATED_SCHEMA_HASH_V1>,body:whole closed record}))). All UUIDs allocated before hashing, no hash/ref of self in body; decimal lengths are strings. Registration hash uses GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_REGISTRATION_HASH_V1 with its complete closed body and same wrapper; descriptor hashes use SHA256 GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_SOURCE_JSON_V1(descriptor) to preserve exact signed-int64 schema bounds. Existing evidence service object hashes/codecs are imported, not redefined. Order: accepted source contracts/type exports + source approval -> registration -> actual source/build objects and attestation -> artifact -> test runner evidence -> conformance -> detached artifact review -> five schema materials -> separate general-report field package provenance/review -> atomic registry publication. Conformance/runner evidence never references future conformance or review; review never references future material/package.

## paragraph:4732

admissionAndErrors: At atomic publication, exact owner/type/id/revision/hash resolve each ref; registration/import tuples and five schema set equal, artifact bytes hashes and lengths verified, trusted signatures/current revocation state valid, runner result PASS, independent author-union exclusion holds. All five emitted refs must share registration/artifact/conformance/review/sourceApproval/import pins. Invalid closed input 422; hidden/unreadable dependency follows native non-disclosure 404; authority denial 403; stale registry/review dependency 409; absent compiler/export/trust evidence NOT_CONFIGURED/503. No partial five-schema publication. Retirement/revocation of accepted dependency disables positive new use under native registry lifecycle and does not rewrite immutable bodies. Existing trust/evidence services are mandatory external prerequisites, not fabricated new authority.

## paragraph:4733

referenceEnvelope: Protected registrar allocates registration id and revisionId before registration hashing. All new records are immutable after acceptance: canonical54 IAMRef uses ownerDomain55.01, objectType equal exact GeneralReportSchemaRegistrationV1, GeneralReportValidatorArtifactV1, GeneralReportSchemaConformanceV1 or GeneralReportArtifactReviewV1, objectId=record.id, revisionId=record.revisionId, contentHash=that record codec digest. GeneratedSchemaMaterial is the sole exception: ownerDomain54.01/objectType SchemaRevision as declared above. Registrar enforces unique owner/type/id/revision, stores exact body and digest together atomically, and resolves exact tuple plus current acceptance/revocation state. New revision allocates revisionId, never changes old body. Native source-object, build-evidence, authority and type-contract references retain their existing registered owners/types and must be verified against their real service; these are not assigned invented source55 types.

## paragraph:4734

registration

## paragraph:4735

GeneralReportSchemaRegistrationV1={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,sourceOwner:55.01,family:GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_BUILD_V3,sourceContractApprovalRef:IAMRef,imports:Import[3],entries:Entry[5]}. Import={slot:NativeRef55|MetricEvidenceBinding|SiProfileBundleInput,sourceTypeContractRef:IAMRef,typePath:Text200,contractHash:Hash64}; contractHash equals exact sourceTypeContractRef.contentHash. Entry={schemaKey:exact five descriptor keys,descriptorHash:Hash64,validatorExport:Text140,codecExport:Text140}. Arrays sortedunique and exactcomplete sets, all fields required, noextras. DescriptorHash is SHA256 of entire descriptor under GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_SOURCE_JSON_V1 with its schemaBody; schemaBytesHash is separately canonical schemaBody hash. Source approval and imports exist before registration. New record id/revision generated once by protected owner before hashing, immutable thereafter. Registration body doesnot reference future build/artifact/review/material.

## paragraph:4736

compilerConstraint

## paragraph:4737

For each imported type, actual compiled $defs shape must equal approved canonical source export under pinned approved codec, including transitive required source types; no substitution of candidate file hash for approval. Actual generated root5 schemas must equal canonical descriptor schemaBody/hash; test runner resolves full type graph and all semantic guards from source owner.

## paragraph:4738

sourceSchemaCodec

## paragraph:4739

GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_SOURCE_JSON_V1: encode the closed schema JSON tree as UTF8 without BOM, object keys sorted by Unicode codepoint (all schema property keys ASCII in this candidate), array order retained, no insignificant whitespace, strings JSON escaped quote/backslash/control only with nonASCII UTF8 retained, integers exact canonical decimal with no plus/leadingzero/exponent/float conversion; booleans/null native tokens. Schema tree contains no floating numeric literals. This is a source-schema-only byte codec, not RFC8785/JCS for arbitrary int64 and not application value codec. Full exact emitted bytes and approved implementation/codechash required; fail rather than round signed-int64 extrema.

## paragraph:4740

exchangeBoundary

## paragraph:4741

Three HTTP schemas now internal phase-tagged REQUEST/RESPONSE exchanges, not new public wire wrappers. Canonical body separate fourth schema. Native mutation commandId+data and six-field P312 receipt retained; GET normalizespath id/queryversion/projection, noGETbody. Exact source handler strips internal phase marker on wire; consumer cannot select response phase or supply trusted context. Fifth schema is server-only GeneralReportContextV3 for root/scope resolver binding; never public request or response.

## paragraph:4742

Sonlu alan kimlikleri ve evre bağlamaları

## paragraph:4743

115 kayıt, 47 iş alanını üç işlem evresine bağlar. Okuma profili 47 READ, oluşturma ve güncelleme profilleri ayrı ayrı 25 WRITE + 3 makbuz kimliği READ içerir. Bir evrenin alan yetkisi diğer evreye taşınmaz. Aynı üst ve alt alanı etkileyen kurallar AND uygulanır. Aşağıdaki kayıtların tümü korunur; yıldız veya sınırsız alan genişlemesi yoktur.

## paragraph:4859

symbolicRefRule

## paragraph:4860

R is a finite typed dependency key resolved to actual accepted canonical54 immutable reference. Not a fabricated UUID or approved revision. Missing exact accepted source/schema/field material blocks publication.

## paragraph:4861

exchangeRule

## paragraph:4862

Internal phase+payload binding contract only, no new publicHTTP envelope. Request normalized from nativeHTTP method/path/query/headers; payload remains native commandId+data for mutations. Response payload is unchanged nativeP312 receipt or newexplicitV3 Detail. No requestWRITE applies during RESPONSE; no responseREAD during REQUEST.

## paragraph:4871

sourcePolicy

## paragraph:4872

parentChildRule: All matching field policies are conjunctive, never first-match/OR. presentation child paths require both report.definition.v3.presentation for canonical body access and corresponding report.presentation.v3 child key. publication children require parent report.control.v3.publication and own leaf READ; read projection may omit a denied leaf but cannot fabricate complete publication approval. Empty object never positive approval evidence. Scalar duplicate metadata/definition paths share exact same field key and value; no side-door metadata access.

## paragraph:4873

defaultWriteRule: Server-materialized create defaults do not grant hidden dataset/source rights. All actual stored default business fields require create-time applicable field WRITE grant; missing field WRITE prevents successful command, never silently writes a hidden field. Intentionally incomplete draft fields omitted by createPresencePolicy remain absent. Later PATCH uses present fields only, no server default injection.

## paragraph:4874

identityRule: projection is a closed requested protocol selector, not business field. report_id/version_id/state/availableActions remain listed registry fields: identity availability requires exact root read permission before dispatch. If required envelope field unreadable, fail nondisclosing not return invalid partial identity response. No wildcard permission inferred from protocol selector.

## paragraph:4875

siBundle: Whole bundle field grant AND exact native SI population/period contributor requirements; outer READ/WRITE never replaces native20 nested profile field ceilings.

## paragraph:4876

controlFields: Request commandId/idempotency/IfMatch/schema selector/path id/query version/projection are native closed protocol constraints, not editable business fields. Response commandId/version/contentHash protected by nativeP312 receipt contract, not new data-field WRITE. If contract acceptance cannot establish exact predecessor receipt authority, mutation activation remains blocked.

## paragraph:4877

fieldDefinitionFamily

## paragraph:4878

Closed exact8 keys {fieldKey:finite47,phase:REQUEST|RESPONSE,wirePath:finite115sourcepath,schemaPath:exact internalexchange path,sourceSchemaRef:IAMRef,capability:READ|WRITE,valueContract:accepted source schema value-type label,atomicContributorGuard:IAMRef to GeneralReportCompoundGuardV3}. Name/type labels not evaluated as code; actual compiledsource schema member and guard handler must be registered and current.
