## paragraph:4902

newScopeBoundary

## paragraph:4903

V3 is explicitly new source-owned TENANT-only function/root context. No claim previous unknown function/scopes accepted. This narrows root grant scope only; every native source company/vessel/purpose/row/field guard still AND. menuEligible=false creates no new visible menu or automatic menu access; existing UI navigation may invoke only exact independently permitted V3 actions. No reuse of SI_SERVICE function/module.

## paragraph:4904

predecessorBranch

## paragraph:4905

Controlled registry sees B0363/B0364/B0365 source catalog identities. If exact accepted effective native action/mapping exists: verify original action identity, source minimumrisk, actor/delegation and allowedfunction/scope fields; never union incompatible variants. Equal compatible new V3 mapping may be accepted in same fullregistry revision. If mismatch (including function namespace/actor/scope/risk), registration fails422 ACTION_VARIANT_CONFLICT; owner must explicitly approve versioned redesign of affected exact native contracts and full grant impact/migration, not silently widenoroverwrite. If predecessor exists only as design UNKNOWN_NOT_VERIFIED catalog: bind historicalsource evidence and bootstrap explicitnewV3contracts; olddefinition remainsdisabled, no inherited grants. Zero or ambiguous native identity rejects. Actual deployment requires handler/exchange/field/source fence evidence. Newfieldkeys have defaultGrant=false; existing humanrole entries not autoexpanded.

## paragraph:4910

acyclicOrder

## paragraph:4911

Independent source-design approval/export acceptance -> five schemas -> source contract -> root resolver/row rule/field definitions/compound guard/native receipt contract -> sourcefieldpolicy -> fieldmappingowner/functionowner/scope/root/risk/actor -> native function and3fieldprofiles ->3owneraction contracts ->3admission mappings -> fullregistry independent review -> runtimeactivation. Sourcecontract uses fieldPolicyKey notfuturepolicyRef; sourcefieldpolicy uses futureprofile logicalkeys notrefs; registrar final atomic consistency checkskeys resolve exact acceptedrecords, never treats key as proof. No approval recursively pins future own dependentmaterial hashes.

## paragraph:4912

resolutionRules

## paragraph:4913

All R references resolved by protected owner bootstrap to actual accepted immutable refs. sourceDesignHash is a derived required slot filled only by approved sourceDesignRef.contentHash, not supplied fake literal; publication rejects sentinel. ApprovedSourceReportAggregateContract and ApprovedSourceDesignRevision are required explicit source accepted input contract types defined by the protected source owner, not asserted existing records. If source owner cannot provide accepted exact producer schema/authority, NOT_CONFIGURED. Their source evidence must cover current native55 root/read/write/receipt semantics; no per-user prompt supplies approval.

## paragraph:4914

materialHashRule

## paragraph:4915

Each NEW owner55 family uses closed preimage {codec:GENERAL_REPORT_V3_OWNER_MATERIAL_HASH_V1,ownerDomain,objectType,objectId,revisionId,body}, actualallocatedUUIDs, immutable body; SHA256 UTF8 RFC8785 for staticownerbodies with no unsafe numeric material values. OwncontentHash external; no future refs. Native54 function/fieldmapping/owneraction/risk/actor codecs unchanged. Schema literalint64 bodies use separate GENERAL_REPORT_SCHEMA_SOURCE_JSON_V1, never genericJCS. Approved source design/aggregate inputs precede newmaterial and cannot reference future ownoutputs.

## paragraph:4916

endpointProducerClosure

## paragraph:4917

New design-only bootstrap emits exact12 ActionDefinition and exact10 EndpointBinding only after required typed resolution slots are supplied from accepted GeneralReportHandlerAcceptanceV3 and compared against exact source tuple/schema. RESOLVE_* strings are template notation, never permitted native payload literal/null/build placeholder; absent handler acceptance is NOT_CONFIGURED and forbids endpoint activation/publication as implemented. Draft design material may remain CONTRACT_READY/DISABLED, no claim endpoint exists. The controlled producer verifies schemaVersion native catalog type and exact value; if native schemaVersion is not this accepted source string, explicit versioned schema/handler type admission required, no coerced value. Accepted existing binding branch retains real build/test pins, source5schema equality and latest owner design validation; no synthetic source acceptance.

contextEpochSemantics

## paragraph:4919

sourceEpoch is NEW GeneralReportContextV3 positive-int64 owner55 root-context generation sequence, not a narrowing/cast of any imported epoch. Allocated under native report transaction and advanced for root/version-selection changes. Native aggregate/version and every contributor source/current permission revision token remain exact private fence evidence; equality of sourceEpoch alone never proves cross-source freshness. On reaching int64 maximum refuse new context generation with503 EPOCH_EXHAUSTED until approved migration, neverwrap/reset/reuse.

## paragraph:4920

Uygulama kabul kapıları

## paragraph:4921

Geliştirici kabulü: kaynak onayı ve gerçek tip dışa aktarımı; beş şemanın doğrulanmış derlemesi; 115 alan bağlaması ve üç eylemin bağımsız sicil incelemesi; eksik/yanlış yetki ve kaynak kesintisi negatif testleri; atomik sürüm/kimlik/tekrar deneme ve güncel kaynak kilitleri; mevcut sürümler için geriye uyumluluk testleri birlikte geçmelidir. Yerel sentetik şema, ifade ve kalite testleri bu gerçek ortam kanıtlarını üretmez.
